country_event = {
	id = 5050
	title = "EVTNAME5050"
	desc = "EVTDESC5050"
	picture = "Administration"
	trigger = {
		accepted_culture = north_german
		NOT = {
			any_owned_province = {
				AND = {
					culture = north_german
					OR = {
						is_colonial = no
						owner = {
							citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.01
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA5050"
		remove_accepted_culture = north_german
	}
}
province_event = {
	id = 5051
	title = "EVTNAME5051"
	desc = "EVTDESC5051"
	trigger = {
		is_primary_culture = no
		is_accepted_culture = yes
		culture = north_german
		total_pops = 5000
		average_militancy = 4
		owner = {
			OR = {
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						culture = north_german
					}
				}
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 12
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 6
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 100
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA5051"
		owner = {
			remove_accepted_culture = north_german
		}
	}
}
province_event = {
	id = 5052
	title = "EVTNAME5052"
	desc = "EVTDESC5052"
	trigger = {
		is_accepted_culture = no
		culture = north_german
		total_pops = 40000
		NOT = {
			average_militancy = 0.4
		}
		OR = {
			is_colonial = no
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 240
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			NOT = {
				average_militancy = 0.2
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			NOT = {
				average_militancy = 0.1
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 5
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 25
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 14
			is_colonial = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA5052"
		owner = {
			add_accepted_culture = north_german
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = 5347
	title = "EVTNAME5347"
	desc = "EVTDESC5347"
	picture = "Administration"
	trigger = {
		accepted_culture = assamese
		NOT = {
			any_owned_province = {
				AND = {
					culture = assamese
					OR = {
						is_colonial = no
						owner = {
							citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.01
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA5347"
		remove_accepted_culture = assamese
	}
}

	id = 5665
	title = "EVTNAME5665"
	desc = "EVTDESC5665"
	picture = "Administration"
	trigger = {
		accepted_culture = wolof
		NOT = {
			any_owned_province = {
				AND = {
					culture = wolof
					OR = {
						is_colonial = no
						owner = {
							citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.01
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA5665"
		remove_accepted_culture = wolof
	}
}
province_event = {
	id = 5666
	title = "EVTNAME5666"
	desc = "EVTDESC5666"
	trigger = {
		is_primary_culture = no
		is_accepted_culture = yes
		culture = wolof
		total_pops = 5000
		average_militancy = 4
		owner = {
			OR = {
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						culture = wolof
					}
				}
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 12
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 6
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 8
		}
		modifier = {

			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
			}
		}

			factor = 100

			}
		}
	}
	option = {

		}
		OR = {

			}
		}

			factor = 0.5
			NOT = {
				average_militancy = 0.2
			}
		}

			}
		}
		modifier = {

			owner = {

			}
		}

			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 14
			is_colonial = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
